HEARING, THIS DAY, ALOK ARADHE J., DELIVERED THE
FOLLOWING:
JUDGMENT
This intra court appeal has been filed against the
order dated 27.03.2019 passed in W.P.No. 42152-
42620/2018.

2. Learned counsel for the parties jointly submit
that the issue involved in this appeal has been settled
by a judgment of the Hon'ble Supreme Court dated
04.11.2022 passed in 'THE EMPLOYEES PROVIDENT
FUND ORGANISATION & ANR ETC.VS SUNIL
KUMAR B.& ORS ETC.', 2022 SCC ONLINE SC
1521.
3. In view of the aforesaid judgment, which is
binding on this court in terms of Article 14 of the

225
Constitution of India, the appeal is disposed of in
terms of the directions contained in the judgment.
Accordingly, the appeal is disposed of. The
pending interlocutory applications, if any, are
disposed of.
